# DDL resolution readiness inventory

## Compact, complete source-indexed register

This documentation-only register covers **1435 mapping IDs**, **1459 occurrences**, **110 additional-operation IDs**, and **8 startup owners**. Every authoritative status is **UNRESOLVED**. `SOURCE EVIDENCE INCOMPLETE` is determined only from the source-semantic checklist in `inventory.json`; it is separate from production evidence. It is not an execution, approval, production characterization, or semantic-resolution claim.

**Initial snapshot (before these readiness files):** HEAD `e03e2acf8f300dee738d3dd5cb8aafcc0da4d6ab`; canonical SHA-256 `643a649989c3658c96ae16d90c003eeeeee542f76d94cb3a8b00f6328002fc60`. The input pins in [inventory.json](inventory.json) were calculated before readiness-file writes.

## Import/call boundary audited first

The only loader used was `loadRepositoryCrosswalk` from `scripts/src/startup-migration-crosswalk.ts`. Its runtime imports are Node `crypto`, `fs`, `path`, `url`, and TypeScript; `production-startup-ddl-inventory` is imported as a type only. The loader reads baseline/canonical text and the static TypeScript parser reads owner files as text. No owner module, API entrypoint, pool, migration runner, database client, test, workflow, network, or application startup was executed.

## Schema and aliases for dependent documents

`inventory.json` has root aliases `mappingIds`, `additionalOperationIds`, `occurrences`, and `objectGroups`; canonical record data is under `records.mappings` and `records.additionalOperations`. Mapping/additional/occurrence IDs are direct root aliases; every tuple inherits root `authoritativeStatus: UNRESOLVED` and `sourceChecklistProfile: SOURCE EVIDENCE INCOMPLETE`; the compact tuple position is the stable join (`mapping:<mappingIds[i]>`, `additional-operation:<additionalOperationIds[i]>`, and `occurrence:<mappingId>:<ordinal>`). Each record lists actual `objectGroup` indexes. An object group is keyed from actual structured `objectIdentity`, or for an additional operation lacking one, from a parsed SQL reference; operational advisory-lock records use their actual SQL lock-resource identifier; a record with neither a structured identity, SQL reference, nor lock resource would remain ungrouped rather than receiving an arbitrary bucket.

### Exact SQL preservation without duplicate/truncated copies

Every occurrence gives its owner-source SHA-256, exact literal/operation line and column, full-literal SHA-256, and immutable `staticCrosswalk.mappings[i].occurrences[j].sourceSql` reference. That field is the complete executable SQL returned by the static loader. The owner source itself is pinned in `inputPins`; no SQL is abbreviated or substituted with a summary. Additional-operation source evidence is likewise pinned with a file SHA-256 and JSON pointer to the existing complete-evidence record.

## Operational/recovery catalog

- **OP-ORDER-BRANCH-FAILURE** — Prove exact reachable branch, source/transaction order, failure propagation and final as well as intermediate postconditions.
- **OP-LOCK-VALIDITY** — Characterize lock/timeout behavior, index or constraint validity, partial completion and repair/recovery boundary.
- **OP-BINDING-SECURITY** — Compare full function/trigger/policy body, binding, privileges, volatility/security/search_path and dependent objects.
- **OP-LOCK-TIMEOUT-TRANSACTION** — Characterize advisory-lock scope, session GUC restoration, transaction/autocommit boundary and failure cleanup.
- **OP-DATA-INVARIANT-COMPENSATION** — Prove affected rows/invariants, concurrent writers, business authorization and compensation/restore plan.
- **RECOVERY-RETRY-NOT-PROOF** — IF [NOT] EXISTS, retry or suppression alone is not rollback/recovery proof; preserve an approved repair/restore boundary.

## Reproducible static projection (no owner execution)

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { loadRepositoryCrosswalk } from "./scripts/src/startup-migration-crosswalk.ts";
const sha256=(p:string)=>createHash("sha256").update(readFileSync(p)).digest("hex");
const { baseline, crosswalk } = loadRepositoryCrosswalk();
const occurrences = crosswalk.mappings.flatMap((m, mi) => m.occurrences.map((o, oi) => ({
  occurrenceId: `occurrence:${m.fingerprint}:${oi + 1}`, mappingId: m.fingerprint,
  owner: o.owner, sourcePath: o.sourcePath, sourcePosition: o.sourcePosition,
  executionOrder: o.executionOrder, executionPath: o.executionPath,
  ownerSourceSha256: sha256(o.sourcePath.split(":")[0]), literalSha256: o.sourceSqlChecksum,
  fullSql: o.sourceSql, // complete literal; do not shorten
})));
if (baseline.owners.length !== 8 || crosswalk.mappings.length !== 1435 ||
    occurrences.length !== 1459 || crosswalk.additionalOperations.length !== 110 ||
    crosswalk.mappings.some(x=>x.status!=="UNRESOLVED") ||
    crosswalk.additionalOperations.some(x=>x.status!=="UNRESOLVED")) throw Error("crosswalk drift");
console.log(JSON.stringify({ occurrences }, null, 2));
```

## Record directory

The complete machine-readable tuple records, individual evidence links/gaps, exact SQL references, branch references, dependencies, postconditions, operational risks, and recovery references are in the JSON registers; `recordEncoding` is the field key. This directory makes every identity directly reviewable without duplicating source SQL.
